## [MINIMAL CASE]

MEASURED minimal trigger: `ProcFs`, positive PID42, metadata ENOENT, public token-aware verdict. Live-holder signal result success or EPERM establishes expected Alive but original never queries it. Recorded token7 included unchanged to match real lock-record path; defect also applies tokenless.

Eliminated DB, engine startup, config, real lock files, process timing, privilege changes and hidepid mount. No live-store writes. This proves faulty classification **conditional on ENOENT for live holder**; it does not prove this host supplies that condition for another UID.

READ reachable consequences:

1. `platform.rs:198-211`: metadata NotFound produces Absent.
2. `SystemProcessProbe::liveness`, `platform.rs:274-280`, passes observation into `decide_liveness`.
3. `platform.rs:145-147`: ProcFs + Absent yields Dead.
4. `operation_lock.rs:123-135`: parsed positive PID whose verdict is Dead misses live-return branch; best-effort `remove_file`, returns None.
5. `engine_lock.rs:249-275` uses `read_live_lock` for stale-owner acquisition. Cross-process operation lock uses same primitive.

READ consequence is conditional on caller access to lock path/file, same PID namespace and ProcFs observability. Actual cross-user lock theft was not executed. No lock deleted for proof.

## [FINDINGS]

### Ranked hypotheses and discrimination

Ranking recorded before subsequent experiments in `.tmp/scratch/issue131/notes.md`.

1. **Confirmed at injected gathering boundary:** NotFound arm equates hidden-live with absent. Prediction: A changes success/EPERM Dead to Alive without losing ESRCH recovery. MEASURED fulfilled. Against: native hidepid not exercised, so environmental trigger remains REPORTED by issue/source comment, not locally measured.
2. **Falsified as primary cause:** pure decision table incorrectly handles unknown-present. Prediction would be A still Dead. MEASURED A returns Alive for unknown-present, existing decision tests pass; table correct for supplied observation. Do not rewrite table.
3. **Falsified for A4 hard case:** current parser selects first delimiter or wrong field. MEASURED original real thread comm `a) b)c` parses correct field22; first-delimiter and index18 mutants fail. Test gap exists, parser defect not reproduced.
4. **Falsified on ordinary same-namespace control only:** signal0 cannot separate live and reaped PID. MEASURED self signal0 succeeds; reaped child absent in proc and syscall returns -1/ESRCH. Cross-user permission/hidepid remains unmeasured.

### A/B competing designs — shared input / expected outputs

| Metadata / signal0 | Original | A: only ESRCH absent | B: every ENOENT unknown |
|---|---|---|---|
| ENOENT / success | Dead, 0 calls | Alive, 1 call | Alive, 0 calls |
| ENOENT / EPERM | Dead, 0 calls | Alive, 1 call | Alive, 0 calls |
| ENOENT / ESRCH | Dead, 0 calls | Dead, 1 call | Alive, 0 calls |
| ENOENT / EINVAL | Dead, 0 calls | Alive, 1 call | Alive, 0 calls |
| ENOENT / EIO | Dead, 0 calls | Alive, 1 call | Alive, 0 calls |
| EACCES / injected ESRCH | Alive, 0 calls | Alive, 0 calls | Alive, 0 calls |
| real self, ordinary proc | Alive | Alive | Alive |
| real reaped child, absent proc | Dead | Dead | Alive |

All cells MEASURED; logs `original-diagnostic__.log`, `A-diagnostic__.log`, `B-diagnostic__.log`. Final A real exited child10671 returned -1 errno3/ESRCH. No nonzero signal sent.

- **A pros:** meets live-holder safety plus existing Linux automatic stale recovery. Cons: one syscall on ENOENT; positive checked PID conversion and immediate errno handling required; conservative unknown can leave lock held.
- **B pros:** less code, no syscall, preserves hidden holder. Cons: loses automatic dead Linux holder recovery; both synthetic ESRCH and actual reaped-child tests fail. Retain as losing conservative alternative, not unsafe design.
- **Decision INFERRED from measured matrix:** A wins given existing dead-holder recovery contract in CONTEXT and callers. Syseng gate still required; diagnosis not design approval.
- **Refutation for A:** a trusted same-PID-namespace hidden live process receiving signal0 ESRCH would invalidate distinction; investigate namespace/security-policy interference before reclaiming. Success and EPERM intentionally collapse to Alive.
- **Inverted world / dissolve code work:** current production path preserving injected live ENOENT would dissolve A3; reproduced opposite. If native environment never permits cross-user sharing, exposure is absent for that deployment, not general primitive proof. If automatic Linux recovery contract is withdrawn, B becomes viable safety-only alternative. A4 remains test-only if parser passes; measured that outcome.

### Signal semantics / PID narrowing

READ: `mem-store/Cargo.toml:15` already declares workspace libc. `mem-profile/src/learning_lock.rs:101-120` is precedent, not code to import: it uses unchecked `pid as libc::pid_t`.

MEASURED x86_64 scratch checked-call recorder:

```text
checked pid=0 target=None result=Err(Kind(InvalidInput)); unchecked=0
checked pid=4294967295 target=None result=Err(Kind(InvalidInput)); unchecked=-1
checked pid=42 target=Some(42) result=Ok(()); unchecked=42
```

READ syscall contract: kill target0 is process-group selector; negative targets address groups / broader sets, so never pass unchecked cast. Scratch does **not** invoke kill with invalid targets. Contract requires signal exactly0: existence/permission probe, no delivery. Success does not set useful errno; inspect `last_os_error()` only immediately after -1. EPERM means insufficient permission, not death. EINVAL/EIO/unknown errors remain unverifiable. ESRCH only absence evidence. Kernel can report success for zombie; conservative Alive acceptable until reaped. No retry/atomic ownership guarantee added.

MEASURED Linux normal self success and reaped-child ESRCH; EPERM/unknown cells synthetic, not real cross-UID probes. Local kill(2) man unavailable; no web needed for diagnosis; authoritative cross-user environment proof deferred to syseng. No privilege/mount changes attempted.

### A4 parser

READ parser `platform.rs:168-172`: last right parenthesis then suffix token19 = field22. Correct for embedded `)` and spaces. Existing test `platform.rs:529-536` repeats same algorithm, weak independence.

MEASURED hard-case uses current test **thread** TID, PR_GET_NAME / PR_SET_NAME with RAII restore; verifies exact `/proc/<tid>/stat` prefix `<tid> (a) b)c) `. Expected field22 selected from exact known fixture prefix and indexed suffix vector, not copied `rsplit_once`/nth expression. Neighbour fields21 and23 asserted different. Example original final hard-case field21=0, field22=58995569, field23=284893184; parsed exact field22. Differing recorded token field21 produces Dead via public `decide_liveness`.

Both mutants fail exact token assertion, Some(0) versus nonzero field22; first delimiter mutant and index18 mutant logs retained. This is GREEN baseline + mutant RED coverage, not fabricated parser baseline RED. Issue's blanket “misparse safe” wording is too broad: None is conservative, but wrong Some token can falsely disagree and produce Dead. Exact-token assertion needed.

## [ROOT CAUSE]

Confirmed classification defect: Linux `observe_process` treats `/proc/<pid>` NotFound alone as proof of death despite source comment acknowledging hidden-live indistinguishability. Decision and reclaim callers trust that false absence. Smallest faulty branch `platform.rs:205`. Native cross-user hidepid reachability is **UNCONFIRMED locally**, REPORTED by issue and documented source caveat. No claim of measured cross-user lock removal.

A4: no parser defect reproduced; missing independent adversarial coverage.

## [FIX PRESCRIPTION]

Coder waits for authorization + syseng gate. Only production file needed: `crates/mem-store/src/platform.rs`, functions `observe_process`, private syscall adapter, same-module tests. No manifest/dependency change, no lock schema/API change, no `learning_lock.rs` edit.

1. Add smallest private Linux `observe_process_with(pid, metadata, start_time, signal0)` closure seam per blueprint. Mechanically move existing gathering body unchanged first; real `observe_process` delegates. Keep PID0 immediate Absent and non-Linux behavior unchanged. Private fixture ProcessProbe invokes **same helper** and existing decision; avoid duplicated match in tests.
2. Add hidden-ENOENT regression before fix. Metadata returns actual NotFound; signal adapter records calls. Through public `ProcessProbe::liveness`, assert success/EPERM/unknown Alive, ESRCH Dead; EACCES metadata stays Alive with zero signal calls; present PID keeps tokens. On unchanged gathering, success/EPERM must fail **verdict assertion**, not missing-helper compile error. This exact input replica already RED; coder must record production-package RED after behavior-preserving seam extraction.
3. Change only NotFound arm: query signal0; only `Err(e)` with `e.raw_os_error() == Some(libc::ESRCH)` returns Absent; every other result `Present { start_time: None }`. Preserve metadata-Ok start-time reads; other metadata failures never call signal0.
4. Production signal adapter checked-converts `u32` to positive `libc::pid_t`; failed conversion returns InvalidInput without syscall. Call `unsafe { libc::kill(target, 0) }`; return Ok on0; otherwise capture last_os_error immediately. No unchecked cast; no shell subprocess. Test actual conversion adapter with recording syscall closure, not separate reimplementation. PID0 no call; u32::MAX no call/Alive; valid positive unchanged target. Current narrowing scratch is design prototype, not production wiring proof.
5. Add hard comm test through `process_start_time(tid)`, RAII thread-name restore, exact-prefix independent field22 and neighbours, mismatched recorded-token Dead. Parser stays unchanged. Kill first-paren and wrong-index mutants in isolated copy. Do not use merely matching reader/writer tokens.
6. Add real self + naturally exiting/reaped child SystemProcessProbe check. Verify missing proc entry before Dead; PID reuse means retry bounded or fixture unavailable, not false bug claim. Only signal0, no child.kill required.
7. Run named tests, package/workspace gates from blueprint after fix. Required mutants: old NotFound arm; every ENOENT unknown; any error absent; unchecked PID cast; first delimiter; wrong field index. Diagnose unrelated failures honestly. Debugger did not run full package/workspace gates.

Runnable first regression model and discrimination scripts above remain scratch examples, not shipping patch. If injected test bypasses common gathering body, reject test as vacuous. No broader abstraction/new public trait/global override needed.

## Cleanup / post-mortem / evidence

MEASURED no source/config changes, no DB/lock writes, no real signals except signal0 to controlled current process or reaped child. PR_SET_NAME affects only fixture test thread and restores through RAII. All adapter instrumentation tagged `[DEBUG-issue131]` under approved `.tmp/scratch/issue131/`; production tree remains clean. Scratch deliberately retained for handoff, never commit. Original repro remains RED by design; no production fix authorized. A replica GREEN demonstrates proposed classification, not shipped correction.

Initial experiment harness bug retained under `adapter-draft/`: A synthetic signal adapter also handled real control, returning default success and making child Alive. Fixed harness to use real checked signal0 only when metadata adapter selects real filesystem; reran full matrix. This was probe defect, not evidence against A. Initial mutant generator assertion failed because search matched ordinary existing test too; narrowed match to exact production parser statement. No failed setup counted as mutant kill.

Prevention: gathering-error tests must distinguish ENOENT live/dead instead of decision-only tables; independent parser oracle plus adversarial actual comm prevents shared-parser false confidence. No architectural rewrite warranted.

Artifacts: `prepare.py`, `run.py`, `parser-tests.rs`, `runs.json`, `environment.json`, `notes.md`, original/A/B and mutant source replicas, raw logs and manifest under `.tmp/scratch/issue131/`. Coder must preserve durable scoped ruling/evidence under `docs/evidence/issue-131/` only when execution authorized; debugger intentionally does not write those production docs.

# DIAGNOSTIC SCRATCH, never commit
Start measured UTC 2026-11-09T08:48:11.863548+00:00; HEAD 24552dbb5c659cfac85ca2ac266fcbaf8702d011; git status clean. Blueprint body sha256 f02d73a05c9e9a897834a589daf9fe1edc2f1047e71a8fa52bbce75032863f0f verified.

Feedback command run: original/Cargo.toml diagnostic::hidden_live; exit101, metadata ENOENT + signal success yielded Absent/Dead, expected Alive, calls0. Actual source gathering/decision/public liveness body retained; only metadata boundary replaced in scratch replica. Native hidepid not measured. Minimal input pid42, recorded7, ProcFs, metadata ENOENT; no DB, lock file or timing needed.

Ranked falsifiable hypotheses BEFORE subsequent probes:
1. NotFound gathering arm wrongly equates hidden with absent. A corroboration should turn success/EPERM from Dead to Alive, preserving ESRCH Dead; B unknown-present should fail ESRCH recovery.
2. Decision/table is defect instead of gathering. If so feeding unknown-present from changed arm would still yield Dead. Existing source predicts opposite.
3. Parser uses first delimiter or wrong field, invalidating token independently. Real thread comm a) b)c with independent field22 should fail existing parser if true; delimiter/index mutants should fail if original correct.
4. Signal-0 unable to distinguish live/dead even normal namespace. Self and reaped-child controls would fail success/ESRCH predictions. Cross-user/hidepid remains unmeasured irrespective of these controls.

Instrumentation prefix [DEBUG-issue131]; all logs/adapters confined .tmp/scratch/issue131. No production edits or lock writes. No real nonzero signal allowed; do not run original test_start_time_parser_selects_stat_field_22 (uses child.kill).

===== .tmp/scratch/issue131/parser-tests.rs =====
// DIAGNOSTIC SCRATCH ONLY. Uses current thread comm; restores even on panic.
#[cfg(test)] mod parser_diagnostic {
    use super::*;
    struct Restore([libc::c_char;16]);
    impl Drop for Restore { fn drop(&mut self) { assert_eq!(unsafe{libc::prctl(libc::PR_SET_NAME,self.0.as_ptr())},0); } }
    #[test] fn hard_comm() {
        let mut old=[0;16];
        assert_eq!(unsafe{libc::prctl(libc::PR_GET_NAME,old.as_mut_ptr())},0);
        let _restore=Restore(old);
        assert_eq!(unsafe{libc::prctl(libc::PR_SET_NAME,c"a) b)c".as_ptr())},0);
        let tid=u32::try_from(unsafe{libc::gettid()}).unwrap();
        let text=std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{tid}/stat")).unwrap();
        // Independent boundary: exact fixture prefix, no delimiter scan/nth19.
        let prefix=format!("{tid} (a) b)c) ");
        let suffix=text.strip_prefix(&prefix).expect("kernel comm matches hard-case fixture");
        let fields: Vec<_>=suffix.split_whitespace().collect();
        let field22: u64=fields[22-3].parse().unwrap();
        let previous: u64=fields[21-3].parse().unwrap();
        let next: u64=fields[23-3].parse().unwrap();
        assert_ne!(field22,previous); assert_ne!(field22,next);
        let got=process_start_time(tid);
        println!("[DEBUG-issue131] tid={tid} comm=a) b)c field21={previous} field22={field22} field23={next} parsed={got:?}");
        assert_eq!(got,Some(field22));
        assert_eq!(decide_liveness(tid,Some(previous),ObservedProcess::Present{start_time:got},ProcObservability::ProcFs),Liveness::Dead);
    }
    fn checked_signal_with(pid:u32, call:impl FnOnce(libc::pid_t)->std::io::Result<()>) -> std::io::Result<()> {
        let target=libc::pid_t::try_from(pid).ok().filter(|p|*p>0).ok_or_else(||std::io::Error::from(std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput))?;
        call(target)
    }
    #[test] fn narrowing() {
        for (pid,expected) in [(0,None),(u32::MAX,None),(42,Some(42))] {
            let mut target=None;
            let result=checked_signal_with(pid, |p|{target=Some(p);Ok(())});
            println!("[DEBUG-issue131] checked pid={pid} target={target:?} result={result:?}; unchecked={}",pid as libc::pid_t);
            assert_eq!(target,expected);
            assert_eq!(result.is_ok(),expected.is_some());
        }
    }
}


===== .tmp/scratch/issue131/prepare.py =====
# DIAGNOSTIC SCRATCH ONLY. Replica alters metadata adapter, never production tree.
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
out=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
src=(root/'crates/mem-store/src/platform.rs').read_text()
assert src.count('match std::fs::metadata(Path::new("/proc").join(pid.to_string())) {')==1
src=src.replace('match std::fs::metadata(Path::new("/proc").join(pid.to_string())) {','match diagnostic_metadata(pid) {')
extra='''
// [DEBUG-issue131] Only filesystem boundary replaced. Original gathering/decision bodies preserved.
thread_local! { static META: std::cell::Cell<Option<i32>> = const { std::cell::Cell::new(None) }; }
fn diagnostic_metadata(pid: u32) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    META.with(|v| match v.get() {
        Some(errno) => Err(std::io::Error::from_raw_os_error(errno)),
        None => std::fs::metadata(format!("/proc/{pid}")).map(|_| ()),
    })
}
thread_local! { static SIGNAL: std::cell::Cell<(Option<i32>,usize)> = const { std::cell::Cell::new((None,0)) }; }
fn diagnostic_signal(pid: u32) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    if META.with(|v|v.get().is_none()) {
        let target=libc::pid_t::try_from(pid).ok().filter(|p|*p>0).ok_or_else(||std::io::Error::from(std::io::ErrorKind::InvalidInput))?;
        let rc=unsafe {libc::kill(target,0)};
        return if rc==0 {Ok(())} else {Err(std::io::Error::last_os_error())};
    }
    SIGNAL.with(|v| { let (errno,n)=v.get(); v.set((errno,n+1));
        println!("[DEBUG-issue131] signal adapter pid={pid} errno={errno:?}");
        errno.map_or(Ok(()), |e| Err(std::io::Error::from_raw_os_error(e)))
    })
}
#[cfg(test)] mod diagnostic {
    use super::*;
    #[test] fn hidden_live() { matrix(false); }
    #[test] fn dead_recovery() { matrix(true); }
    fn matrix(dead_only: bool) {
        let mut failures=Vec::new();
        for (label,errno,expected) in [("success",None,Liveness::Alive),("EPERM",Some(libc::EPERM),Liveness::Alive),("ESRCH",Some(libc::ESRCH),Liveness::Dead),("EINVAL",Some(libc::EINVAL),Liveness::Alive),("EIO",Some(libc::EIO),Liveness::Alive)] {
            if dead_only != (expected==Liveness::Dead) {continue;}
            META.with(|v|v.set(Some(libc::ENOENT)));
            SIGNAL.with(|v|v.set((errno,0)));
            let got=SystemProcessProbe.liveness(42,Some(7));
            let observed=if got==Liveness::Dead {"Absent/Dead"} else {"unknown-present/Alive"};
            println!("[DEBUG-issue131] metadata=ENOENT signal={label} observation={observed:?} verdict={got:?} calls={}",SIGNAL.with(|v|v.get().1));
            if got != expected { failures.push((label,got,expected)); }
        }
        assert!(failures.is_empty(),"ENOENT verdict mismatches: {failures:?}");
    }
    #[test] fn metadata_permission_error() {
        META.with(|v|v.set(Some(libc::EACCES)));
        SIGNAL.with(|v|v.set((Some(libc::ESRCH),0)));
        assert_eq!(SystemProcessProbe.liveness(42,Some(7)),Liveness::Alive);
        assert_eq!(SIGNAL.with(|v|v.get().1),0);
        println!("[DEBUG-issue131] metadata=EACCES signal=ESRCH verdict=Alive calls=0");
    }
    #[test] fn real_controls() {
        META.with(|v|v.set(None));
        let me=std::process::id();
        assert_eq!(SystemProcessProbe.liveness(me,process_start_time(me)),Liveness::Alive);
        assert_eq!(unsafe{libc::kill(libc::pid_t::try_from(me).unwrap(),0)},0);
        let mut child=std::process::Command::new("true").spawn().unwrap();
        let pid=child.id(); child.wait().unwrap();
        assert!(!std::path::Path::new(&format!("/proc/{pid}")).exists(),"PID reused; fixture unavailable");
        let rc=unsafe{libc::kill(libc::pid_t::try_from(pid).unwrap(),0)};
        let err=std::io::Error::last_os_error();
        println!("[DEBUG-issue131] self={me} exited-child={pid} kill0_rc={rc} errno={err:?}");
        assert_eq!(rc,-1); assert_eq!(err.raw_os_error(),Some(libc::ESRCH));
        assert_eq!(SystemProcessProbe.liveness(pid,None),Liveness::Dead);
    }
}
'''
arm='Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => ObservedProcess::Absent,'
assert src.count(arm)==1
for mode in ['original','A','B']:
    text=src
    if mode=='A':
        text=text.replace(arm,'''Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            match diagnostic_signal(pid) {
                Err(e) if e.raw_os_error() == Some(libc::ESRCH) => ObservedProcess::Absent,
                _ => ObservedProcess::Present { start_time: None },
            }
        },''')
    if mode=='B': text=text.replace(arm,'Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => ObservedProcess::Present { start_time: None },')
    d=out/mode; (d/'src').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    (d/'Cargo.toml').write_text((out/'Cargo.toml').read_text().replace('issue131-probe','issue131-'+mode.lower()))
    (d/'src/lib.rs').write_text(text+extra)
print('[DEBUG-issue131] source sha256',hashlib.sha256((root/'crates/mem-store/src/platform.rs').read_bytes()).hexdigest())


===== .tmp/scratch/issue131/run.py =====
# DIAGNOSTIC SCRATCH ONLY. Captures every exit; expected RED does not abort experiment.
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,os,datetime,json
base=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
root=base.parents[2]
subprocess.run(['python3',str(base/'prepare.py')],check=True)
extra=(base/'parser-tests.rs').read_text()
for mode in ['original','A','B']:
    p=base/mode/'src/lib.rs'; p.write_text(p.read_text()+extra)
for mode,old,new in [('first-paren',"let after_comm = stat.rsplit_once(')')?.1;","let after_comm = stat.split_once(')')?.1;"),('index18','split_whitespace().nth(19)','split_whitespace().nth(18)')]:
    d=base/mode; (d/'src').mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    (d/'Cargo.toml').write_text((base/'original/Cargo.toml').read_text().replace('issue131-original','issue131-'+mode))
    src=(base/'original/src/lib.rs').read_text(); assert src.count(old)==1
    (d/'src/lib.rs').write_text(src.replace(old,new))
env=dict(os.environ,CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(base/'target'))
runs=[]
for mode,selector in [('original','diagnostic::'),('A','diagnostic::'),('B','diagnostic::'),('original','parser_diagnostic::'),('first-paren','parser_diagnostic::hard_comm'),('index18','parser_diagnostic::hard_comm'),('original','tests::test_decide_liveness'),('original','tests::test_system_probe_reads_its_own_start_time_token')]:
    cmd=['cargo','test','--quiet','--offline','--manifest-path',str(base/mode/'Cargo.toml'),selector,'--','--nocapture']
    start=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    result=subprocess.run(cmd,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,env=env,text=True)
    name=mode+'-'+selector.replace(':','_')+'.log'; (base/name).write_text(result.stdout)
    row=dict(start=start,end=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),command=cmd,exit=result.returncode,log=name)
    runs.append(row); print(json.dumps(row),flush=True); print(result.stdout[-7000:],flush=True)
(base/'runs.json').write_text(json.dumps(runs,indent=2))

===== .tmp/scratch/issue131-production/run_checks.py =====
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess, datetime, json, os
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE = ROOT / '.tmp/scratch/issue131-production'
def run(name, args, cwd=ROOT, env=None):
    start = subprocess.check_output(['date','-u','+%FT%TZ'], text=True).strip()
    with (BASE / (name + '.log')).open('w') as log:
        result = subprocess.run(args, cwd=cwd, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    end = subprocess.check_output(['date','-u','+%FT%TZ'], text=True).strip()
    record = dict(name=name, command=args, cwd=str(cwd), target=(env or {}).get('CARGO_TARGET_DIR'), start=start, end=end, exit=result.returncode)
    with (BASE / 'checks.jsonl').open('a') as out: out.write(json.dumps(record)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(record), flush=True)
    print('\n'.join((BASE/(name+'.log')).read_text().splitlines()[-35:]), flush=True)
    return result.returncode
if __name__ == '__main__':
    import sys, tarfile, io
    if sys.argv[1] == 'gates':
        commands = [
          ('P-final', ['cargo','test','-p','mem-store','--lib','platform::tests::','--','--nocapture']),
          ('package', ['cargo','test','-p','mem-store']),
          ('workspace', ['cargo','test','--workspace']),

# Opsec Audit Report — opsec-issue131-r5

## Run Metadata

- **Started**: 2026-11-09T10:15:00Z
- **Completed**: 2026-11-09T10:35:00Z
- **Run Status**: audit_complete

## Posture Confirmation
- Report-only: `edit: deny` enforced — no target modifications
- Spawn-proof: `subagent: deny` with `explore: allow` only — serial execution
- No target-controlled execution — source-only analysis per fleet policy
- Validators: both pass (findings.json, coverage-ledger.json)

## Coverage Summary
| Unit | Surface | Boundary | Attack Class | Status |
|------|---------|----------|--------------|--------|
| 1 | ProcObservability::current | host-classification | config-plane | covered |
| 2 | SystemProcessProbe::liveness | production-probe-integration | resource-exhaustion | covered |
| 3 | decide_liveness | lock-reclamation-decision | policy-matching | covered |
| 4 | observe_process_with | host-observability | state-tracking | covered |
| 5 | read_proc_start_time | procfs-parser | parser-bounds | covered |
| 6 | signal_zero | unsafe-libc-boundary | memory-safety | covered |
| 7 | signal_zero_with | unsafe-libc-boundary | memory-safety | covered |

**Total units**: 7 planned, 7 covered, 0 incomplete

## Findings Summary
| Verdict | Count |
|---------|-------|
| confirmed | 0 |
| needs_validation | 0 |
| rejected | 0 |

**No security vulnerabilities confirmed.** The audited code correctly implements fail-closed liveness semantics for stale lock detection with PID reuse protection via start-time tokens.

## Key Audit Observations

### 1. Unsafe Libc Boundary (signal_zero_with, signal_zero)
- **PID conversion**: `libc::pid_t::try_from(pid)` with `.filter(|target| *target > 0)` correctly rejects PID 0 and unrepresentable values (u32::MAX on platforms where pid_t is i32), returning `InvalidInput`.
- **Unsafe block**: `libc::kill(target, 0)` called only with validated positive PID and signal 0. SAFETY comment accurately describes adapter guarantees.
- **Errno handling**: `std::io::Error::last_os_error()` captured immediately after failed syscall — correct.

### 2. Procfs Parser (read_proc_start_time)
- **Comm field parsing**: Uses `rsplit_once(')')` to find last `)` in comm field, then token index 19 (field 22) for starttime. Validated by `test_start_time_parser_handles_comm_with_spaces_and_right_parens` with real comm containing `)`.
- **Error propagation**: Returns `Option<u64>` — `None` on any read/parse failure, treated conservatively as "alive" by decision logic.

### 3. Host Observability (observe_process_with)
- **PID 0**: Short-circuits to `Absent` → `Dead` (null ID never a holder).
- **Metadata Ok**: Reads start_time token → `Present{Some(_)}` or `Present{None}`.
- **NotFound + signal0**: Only `ESRCH` yields `Absent` (Dead); other errors → `Present{None}` (Alive) — hidepid conservative.
- **Other errors (EACCES, EIO, EPERM)**: Conservative `Present{None}` (Alive) — never steal a possibly-live lock.

### 4. Liveness Decision (decide_liveness)
Complete decision table implemented:
| Observability | PID | Recorded Token | Observed | Verdict |
|---------------|-----|----------------|----------|---------|
| any | 0 | any | any | Dead |
| Unobservable | >0 | any | any | Alive (fail-closed) |
| ProcFs | >0 | any | Absent | Dead |
| ProcFs | >0 | absent | Present{Some(_)} | Alive |
| ProcFs | >0 | Some(_) | Present{None} | Alive |
| ProcFs | >0 | Some(t) | Present{Some(t)} | Alive |
| ProcFs | >0 | Some(t) | Present{Some(other)} | Dead (PID reuse) |

All 7 cells covered by tests including cross-platform (`test_decide_liveness_both_directions_per_platform`) and PID reuse (`test_decide_liveness_pid_reuse_is_dead_on_mismatch`).

### 5. Host Classification (ProcObservability::current)
- Linux + accessible `/proc/self/stat` → `ProcFs`
- Stripped container (no `/proc/self/stat`) or non-Linux → `Unobservable` (fail-closed)
- Correctly prevents lock theft in containers without procfs

### 6. Production Integration (SystemProcessProbe)
- `is_alive`: Delegates to `decide_liveness` with `recorded_start: None` (existence only)
- `liveness`: Full token-aware verdict via `decide_liveness` + `observe_process` + `ProcObservability::current`
- Consumed by `read_live_lock` (operation_lock.rs:126) and `acquire_turso_operation_lock` (records own start_time via `LockRecord::for_this_process`)
- Validated by cross-process tests: `test_stale_lock_from_dead_pid_is_reclaimed` (reaped child + ESRCH → reclaimed) and `test_unrepresentable_holder_pid_is_not_reclaimed` (u32::MAX → retained)

## Mutation Testing Evidence (from prior wave)
Six distinct exact-input mutation kills accepted:
- M1: old NotFound Absent mutant (historical)
- M3: any signal error Absent mutant (historical)
- M5: first-delimiter parser mutant (historical)
- M6: wrong index18 parser mutant (historical)
- M2-ESRCH: fresh ESRCH-first mutant (isolated)
- M4-max: fresh u32::MAX-first mutant (isolated)

Plus invalid-holder mutant (separate seam) killed. All mutations target exact required inputs and fail as expected.

## Disclosure: Confirmed Definition

Never a runtime-demonstrated result. Runtime-decisive facts default to `needs_validation` with paste-pack plan. This run found no confirmed vulnerabilities.
